(.venv) foreach ($pattern in $patterns) {
    Write-Host "`n--- SEARCH: $pattern ---" -ForegroundColor Yellow

    $matches = $files | Select-String -SimpleMatch -Pattern $pattern

    if ($matches) {
        $matches | ForEach-Object {
            Write-Host "`nFILE: $($_.Path)" -ForegroundColor Green
            Write-Host "LINE: $($_.LineNumber)"
            Write-Host $_.Line.Trim()
        }
    }
    else {
        Write-Host "No matches found."
    }
}
